        Красивое описание IP (для логов).
        """
        if not ip:
            return "Нет IP"
        if ip == self.lt_ip:
            return "Литва"
        if ip == self.nl_ip:
            return "Нидерланды"
        
        # Для умного выбора определяем локацию по IP
        if hasattr(self, 'lt_ip') and self.lt_ip and ip == self.lt_ip:
            return "Литва (умный выбор)"
        if hasattr(self, 'nl_ip') and self.nl_ip and ip == self.nl_ip:
            return "Нидерланды (умный выбор)"
            
        return "Авто-выбор"

    def get_available_ips(self) -> list[str]:
        """Возвращает список доступных IP"""
        return self.pool.copy()

    def is_smart_selector_active(self) -> bool:
        """Проверяет, активен ли умный селектор"""
        return self.selector_ready and self.selector is not None




















from __future__ import annotations

import os
import random
from typing import Literal, Optional, List

LocationCode = Literal["auto", "lt", "nl"]


def _parse_ips_from_env() -> List[str]:
    """
    Читает KINGLINE_DNS_IPS и возвращает список IP.
    Пример в .env:
        KINGLINE_DNS_IPS=195.35.56.165,195.179.193.193
    """
    raw = os.getenv("KINGLINE_DNS_IPS", "")
    parts = [p.strip() for p in raw.replace(";", ",").split(",") if p.strip()]
    return parts


class DNSBalancer:
    """
    Балансер IP по локации пользователя.

    Локации:
      - 'lt'   -> 🇱🇹 Литва
      - 'nl'   -> 🇳🇱 Нидерланды
      - 'auto' -> случайный выбор между доступными IP.

    IP берём так:
      1) Если заданы KINGLINE_LT_IP / KINGLINE_NL_IP — используем их.
      2) Иначе берём из KINGLINE_DNS_IPS:
         первый IP -> Литва, второй -> Нидерланды (если есть).
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        ips = _parse_ips_from_env()

        self.lt_ip = os.getenv("KINGLINE_LT_IP") or (ips[0] if len(ips) > 0 else "")
        self.nl_ip = os.getenv("KINGLINE_NL_IP") or (ips[1] if len(ips) > 1 else "")

        # Пул IP для авто-выбора
        self.pool: List[str] = [ip for ip in (self.lt_ip, self.nl_ip) if ip] or ips

    def choose_ip(self, location: LocationCode = "auto") -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Вернёт IP для данной локации.
        'auto' -> случайный выбор между доступными IP.
        """
        if location == "lt" and self.lt_ip:
            return self.lt_ip
        if location == "nl" and self.nl_ip:
            return self.nl_ip

        # auto или неизвестное значение
        if not self.pool:
            return None
        return random.choice(self.pool)

    def describe_ip(self, ip: str | None) -> str:
        """
        Красивое описание IP (для логов).
        """
        if not ip:
            return "Нет IP"
        if ip == self.lt_ip:
            return "Литва"
        if ip == self.nl_ip:
            return "Нидерланды"
        return "Неизвестная локация"






























from __future__ import annotations

import os
from typing import Optional, List, Tuple

from db import SubscriptionDB

try:
    # если dns_balancer лежит в том же пакете dns/
    from .dns_balancer import DNSBalancer
except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover
    # fallback, если запускать как скрипт: python dns/user_locator.py
    from dns_balancer import DNSBalancer


class DNSUserLocator:
    """
    Класс, который связывает:
      - доменное имя с sub_id,
      - sub_id с location_pref в БД,
      - location_pref с конкретным IP через DNSBalancer.

    Основной публичный метод: choose_ip_for_qname(qname) -> (ip, loc_pref, loc_name, sub_id)
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        db_path: Optional[str] = None,
        domains: Optional[List[str]] = None,
    ) -> None:
        # Путь к БД; по умолчанию тот же, что использует бот
        db_path = db_path or os.getenv("DB_PATH", "vpn_bot.db")
        self.db = SubscriptionDB(db_path)

        # Список доменов, для которых мы ожидаем sub_id.<domain>
        if domains is not None:
            doms = domains
        else:
            raw = os.getenv("KINGLINE_DNS_DOMAINS", "")
            doms = [d for d in raw.replace(";", ",").split(",") if d.strip()]

        self.domains: List[str] = [
            d.strip().strip(".").lower() for d in doms if d.strip()
        ]

        # Балансер IP по локации (lt/nl/auto)
        self.balancer = DNSBalancer()

    # ===== Вспомогательные методы =====

    def _extract_sub_id_from_qname(self, qname: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Пытаемся вытащить sub_id из имени вида:

            <sub_id>.sub.kingline.online

        где DOMAINS включает 'sub.kingline.online'.

        Если имя просто 'sub.kingline.online' — возвращаем None (глобальный авто-режим).
        """
        name = qname.rstrip(".").lower()

        if not self.domains:
            # нет ограничений по доменам — не пытаемся вытаскивать sub_id
            return None

        for d in self.domains:
            base = d  # например: 'sub.kingline.online'
            if name == base:
                # просто sub.kingline.online — без персонального sub_id
                return None
            if name.endswith("." + base):
                # например: wanderlusthi-XXX.sub.kingline.online
                prefix = name[: -(len(base) + 1)]  # всё, что слева от ".base"
                if not prefix:
                    return None
                # берём ПОСЛЕДНИЙ label как sub_id (на случай a.b.<sub_id>.sub.domain)
                sub_id = prefix.split(".")[-1]
                return sub_id or None

        return None

    def _get_location_by_sub_id(self, sub_id: str) -> str:
        """
        Вернуть location_pref ('auto'/'lt'/'nl') по sub_id.
        Если ничего нет / ошибка / мусор — вернуть 'auto'.
        """
        if not sub_id:
            return "auto"

        try:
            loc = self.db.get_location_by_sub_id(sub_id)
        except Exception:
            return "auto"

        return loc if loc in ("auto", "lt", "nl") else "auto"

    # ===== Публичный метод =====

    def choose_ip_for_qname(self, qname: str) -> Tuple[Optional[str], str, str, str | None]:
        """
        Основной метод, который будет вызываться из DNSHandler.

        Возвращает кортеж:
            (ip, loc_pref, loc_name, sub_id)

        где:
          - ip       — выбранный IP (или None, если не получилось)
          - loc_pref — 'auto' / 'lt' / 'nl' (что в БД у пользователя)
          - loc_name — красивая подпись ('Литва', 'Нидерланды', 'Неизвестная локация')
          - sub_id   — вытащенный sub_id (или None)
        """
        # 1) достаём sub_id из qname
        sub_id = self._extract_sub_id_from_qname(qname)

        # 2) по sub_id узнаём location_pref
        if sub_id:
            loc_pref = self._get_location_by_sub_id(sub_id)
        else:
            loc_pref = "auto"

        # 3) через балансер выбираем IP
        ip = self.balancer.choose_ip(loc_pref)

        # 4) человекочитаемое название локации (для логов)
        loc_name = self.balancer.describe_ip(ip) if ip else "Нет IP"

        return ip, loc_pref, loc_name, sub_id


















































from __future__ import annotations

import os
import logging
import socketserver
import threading
from typing import Optional

from dnslib import DNSRecord, QTYPE, RR, A

# ---------- Загрузка отдельного .env из папки dns ----------

try:
    from dotenv import load_dotenv  # тот же пакет, что у тебя в config.py
except ImportError:
    load_dotenv = None

BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(__file__)
DNS_ENV_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, ".env")

if load_dotenv is not None and os.path.exists(DNS_ENV_PATH):
    load_dotenv(DNS_ENV_PATH)

# Балансер локаций
try:
    from .dns_balancer import DNSBalancer  # если файл в том же пакете dns/
except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover
    from dns_balancer import DNSBalancer   # fallback, если запускаешь как скрипт

# Локатор пользователя по sub_id
try:
    from .user_locator import DNSUserLocator
except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover
    from user_locator import DNSUserLocator

BALANCER = DNSBalancer()

# ---------- Конфиг из окружения (dns/.env и общий .env) ----------

RAW_IPS = os.getenv("KINGLINE_DNS_IPS", "")
IPS = [
    part.strip()
    for part in RAW_IPS.replace(";", ",").split(",")
    if part.strip()
]

RAW_DOMAINS = os.getenv("KINGLINE_DNS_DOMAINS", "")
DOMAINS = [
    d.strip().strip(".").lower()
    for d in RAW_DOMAINS.replace(";", ",").split(",")
    if d.strip()
]

PANEL_DOMAIN = os.getenv("KINGLINE_DNS_PANEL_DOMAIN", "").strip().strip(".").lower()
PANEL_IP = os.getenv("KINGLINE_DNS_PANEL_IP", "").strip()

TTL = int(os.getenv("KINGLINE_DNS_TTL", "30"))

# Апстрим-DNS для всех «чужих» доменов
UPSTREAM_DNS = os.getenv("DNS_UPSTREAM", "1.1.1.1").strip()
UPSTREAM_PORT = int(os.getenv("DNS_UPSTREAM_PORT", "53"))

LOG_LEVEL = os.getenv("DNS_LOG_LEVEL", "INFO").upper()
logging.basicConfig(
    level=getattr(logging, LOG_LEVEL, logging.INFO),
    format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | dns_server | %(message)s",
)
logger = logging.getLogger("dns_server")

if not IPS:
    # если список IP не задан, берём из BALANCER.pool (на всякий случай)
    IPS = BALANCER.pool

if not IPS:
    logger.warning(
        "KINGLINE_DNS_IPS не задана или пуста — сервер будет отвечать NXDOMAIN для наших доменов."
    )
else:
    logger.info("Загружено %d IP для балансировки: %s", len(IPS), ", ".join(IPS))

if DOMAINS:
    logger.info("Ограничиваемся доменами: %s", ", ".join(DOMAINS))
else:
    logger.info("KINGLINE_DNS_DOMAINS не заданы — наши правила применяются ко ВСЕМ A-запросам.")

if PANEL_DOMAIN and PANEL_IP:
    logger.info(
        "Панель %s всегда будет резолвиться в %s (без балансировки)",
        PANEL_DOMAIN,
        PANEL_IP,
    )
else:
    logger.info(
        "Панельный домен / IP не заданы (KINGLINE_DNS_PANEL_DOMAIN / KINGLINE_DNS_PANEL_IP)."
    )

# Инициализируем локатор пользователей
DB_PATH = os.getenv("DB_PATH", "vpn_bot.db")
USER_LOCATOR = DNSUserLocator(db_path=DB_PATH, domains=DOMAINS)
logger.info("DNSUserLocator инициализирован (DB_PATH=%s)", DB_PATH)


def _match_domain(qname: str) -> bool:
    """
    Проверяем, подпадает ли имя под список доменов.
    Если DOMAINS пуст — считаем, что матчим всё.
    """
    if not DOMAINS:
        return True
    name = qname.rstrip(".").lower()
    for d in DOMAINS:
        if name == d or name.endswith("." + d):
            return True
    return False


def _is_panel_domain(qname: str) -> bool:
    """
    Проверяем, относится ли имя к домену панели.

    ВАЖНО: панель — это только СТРОГОЕ совпадение с PANEL_DOMAIN.
    Поддомены (типа <sub_id>.sub.kingline.online) не считаем панелью,
    чтобы они шли через USER_LOCATOR/балансер.
    """
    if not PANEL_DOMAIN:
        return False
    name = qname.rstrip(".").lower()
    return name == PANEL_DOMAIN  # без endswith("." + PANEL_DOMAIN)!


def _forward_to_upstream(request: DNSRecord) -> Optional[bytes]:
    """
    Форвардим DNS-запрос целиком на апстрим (1.1.1.1 / 8.8.8.8 и т.п.).
    Возвращаем сырые байты ответа или None при ошибке.
    """
    if not UPSTREAM_DNS:
        return None

    try:
        qname = str(request.q.qname)
        logger.info("↪️ Форвардим запрос %s на апстрим %s:%d", qname, UPSTREAM_DNS, UPSTREAM_PORT)
        resp_bytes = request.send(UPSTREAM_DNS, UPSTREAM_PORT, timeout=2)
        return resp_bytes
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("Ошибка при форвардинге на %s:%d: %s", UPSTREAM_DNS, UPSTREAM_PORT, e)
        return None


class DNSHandler(socketserver.BaseRequestHandler):
    def handle(self):
        data, sock = self.request
        client_ip, client_port = self.client_address

        try:
            request = DNSRecord.parse(data)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Не удалось распарсить запрос от %s: %s", client_ip, e)
            return

        q = request.q
        qname = str(q.qname)
        qtype = QTYPE.get(q.qtype, "UNKNOWN")

        logger.info("📥 Запрос от %s:%s — %s %s", client_ip, client_port, qname, qtype)

        # --- Наши домены: включаем магию с sub_id и локациями ---
        if qtype == "A" and _match_domain(qname):
            reply = request.reply()
            # авторитетный ответ, рекурсии не даём (для наших доменов)
            reply.header.aa = 1
            reply.header.ra = 0

            try:
                # 1) Статический IP для панели (если задан)
                if PANEL_DOMAIN and PANEL_IP and _is_panel_domain(qname):
                    ip = PANEL_IP
                    logger.info(
                        "📤 Ответ для ПАНЕЛИ %s (client=%s): статический IP %s (TTL=%s)",
                        qname,
                        client_ip,
                        ip,
                        TTL,
                    )
                    reply.add_answer(
                        RR(
                            rname=qname,
                            rtype=QTYPE.A,
                            rclass=1,
                            ttl=TTL,
                            rdata=A(ip),
                        )
                    )

                # 2) Все остальные: sub_id + user_locator + балансер
                elif IPS:
                    try:
                        result = USER_LOCATOR.choose_ip_for_qname(qname)

                        # Поддерживаем и (ip, loc_pref, loc_name),
                        # и (ip, loc_pref, loc_name, sub_id)
                        if not isinstance(result, tuple):
                            raise ValueError(
                                f"USER_LOCATOR вернул не tuple: {type(result)!r}"
                            )

                        if len(result) == 3:
                            ip, loc_pref, loc_name = result
                            sub_id_for_log = None
                        elif len(result) >= 4:
                            ip, loc_pref, loc_name, sub_id_for_log = (
                                result[0],
                                result[1],
                                result[2],
                                result[3],
                            )
                        else:
                            raise ValueError(
                                f"USER_LOCATOR вернул tuple странной длины: {len(result)}"
                            )

                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.exception(
                            "Ошибка в USER_LOCATOR для %s: %s — откатываемся к auto/первому IP",
                            qname,
                            e,
                        )
                        ip = IPS[0] if IPS else None
                        loc_pref = "auto"
                        loc_name = BALANCER.describe_ip(ip) if ip else "Нет IP"
                        sub_id_for_log = None

                    # fallback, если локатор вернул None
                    if not ip:
                        if IPS:
                            ip = IPS[0]
                            loc_pref = "auto"
                            loc_name = BALANCER.describe_ip(ip)
                            sub_id_for_log = None
                        else:
                            logger.info(
                                "NXDOMAIN для %s (client=%s): нет IP в пуле IPS даже для fallback",
                                qname,
                                client_ip,
                            )
                            reply.header.rcode = 3  # NXDOMAIN

                    if ip:
                        logger.info(
                            "📤 Ответ для %s (client=%s, loc_pref=%s, sub_id=%s): выбран IP %s (%s) (TTL=%s)",
                            qname,
                            client_ip,
                            loc_pref,
                            sub_id_for_log,
                            ip,
                            loc_name,
                            TTL,
                        )
                        reply.add_answer(
                            RR(
                                rname=qname,
                                rtype=QTYPE.A,
                                rclass=1,
                                ttl=TTL,
                                rdata=A(ip),
                            )
                        )

                else:
                    # Нет IP в пуле
                    logger.info(
                        "NXDOMAIN для %s (client=%s): нет IP в пуле IPS",
                        qname,
                        client_ip,
                    )
                    reply.header.rcode = 3  # NXDOMAIN

            except Exception as e:
                # Любая ошибка в нашей логике → логируем и шлём NXDOMAIN, чтобы не вешать клиента
                logger.exception("Исключение при обработке нашего домена %s: %s", qname, e)
                reply = request.reply()
                reply.header.aa = 1
                reply.header.ra = 0
                reply.header.rcode = 3  # NXDOMAIN

            try:
                sock.sendto(reply.pack(), self.client_address)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("Ошибка отправки ответа клиенту %s: %s", client_ip, e)
            return

        # --- НЕ наши домены: форвардим на апстрим и возвращаем как есть ---
        try:
            upstream_resp = _forward_to_upstream(request)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("Исключение при форвардинге %s: %s", qname, e)
            upstream_resp = None

        if upstream_resp is not None:
            try:
                sock.sendto(upstream_resp, self.client_address)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("Ошибка отправки форварженного ответа клиенту %s: %s", client_ip, e)
            return

        # Если до сюда дошли — ни апстрима, ни нашего ответа → NXDOMAIN
        logger.info(
            "NXDOMAIN (без апстрима) для %s (client=%s): тип=%s",
            qname,
            client_ip,
            qtype,
        )
        fallback = request.reply()
        fallback.header.aa = 1
        fallback.header.ra = 0
        fallback.header.rcode = 3  # NXDOMAIN
        try:
            sock.sendto(fallback.pack(), self.client_address)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Ошибка отправки fallback-ответа клиенту %s: %s", client_ip, e)


class ThreadedUDPServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.UDPServer):
    allow_reuse_address = True


DNS_SERVER: ThreadedUDPServer | None = None
DNS_THREAD: threading.Thread | None = None


def create_dns_server() -> ThreadedUDPServer:
    """
    Создаёт (один раз) экземпляр DNS-сервера и возвращает его.
    """
    global DNS_SERVER

    if DNS_SERVER is not None:
        return DNS_SERVER

    host = os.getenv("DNS_BIND_HOST", "0.0.0.0")
    port = int(os.getenv("DNS_BIND_PORT", "53"))

    DNS_SERVER = ThreadedUDPServer((host, port), DNSHandler)
    logger.info(
        "DNS-балансер инициализирован на %s:%d (upstream=%s:%d)",
        host,
        port,
        UPSTREAM_DNS,
        UPSTREAM_PORT,
    )
    return DNS_SERVER


def run_dns_server_blocking():
    """
    Блокирующий запуск (для самостоятельного запуска как скрипта).
    """
    server = create_dns_server()
    logger.info("DNS-балансер запущен (blocking mode)")
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        logger.info("Останавливаем DNS-сервер (Ctrl+C)")
    finally:
        server.server_close()
        logger.info("DNS-сервер остановлен")


def start_dns_server_in_background():
    """
    Запускает DNS-сервер в отдельном потоке (НЕ блокирует основной поток).
    Удобно вызывать из main.py бота.
    """
    global DNS_THREAD

    server = create_dns_server()

    # если уже запущен — ничего не делаем
    if DNS_THREAD is not None and DNS_THREAD.is_alive():
        return server, DNS_THREAD

    DNS_THREAD = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    DNS_THREAD.start()
    logger.info("DNS-балансер запущен в фоне (thread)")
    return server, DNS_THREAD


def stop_dns_server():
    """
    Аккуратная остановка сервера (используется из main.py).
    """
    global DNS_SERVER, DNS_THREAD

    if DNS_SERVER is not None:
        try:
            DNS_SERVER.shutdown()
            DNS_SERVER.server_close()
            logger.info("DNS-балансер корректно остановлен")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Ошибка при остановке DNS-сервера: %s", e)
        finally:
            DNS_SERVER = None

    if DNS_THREAD is not None:
        try:
            DNS_THREAD.join(timeout=1.0)
        except Exception:
            pass
        DNS_THREAD = None


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_dns_server_blocking()






import json
import uuid as uuid_lib
import aiohttp
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple


class XUIClient:
    """
    Клиент для API 3x-ui / 3X-UI под бота Kingline.

    Реализует:
      - list_inbounds()
      - add_client_append(...)
      - delete_user_clients(...)
      - restart_xray()

    base_url: например, https://vpn.kingline.online/panel
    Авторизация: через Cookie (XUI_COOKIE).
    """

    def __init__(self, base_url: str, cookie: str, timeout: int = 20, logger_=None):
        self.base = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.cookie = cookie
        self.timeout = timeout
        self.log = logger_ or self._dummy_logger()
        self._session: Optional[aiohttp.ClientSession] = None

        self._headers = {
            "Accept": "application/json, text/plain, */*",
            "X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest",
            "Cookie": self.cookie,
            "User-Agent": "kingline-bot/1.0",
        }

    # ========== context manager ==========

    async def __aenter__(self):
        self._session = aiohttp.ClientSession(headers=self._headers)
        return self

    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
        if self._session:
            await self._session.close()
            self._session = None

    # ========== internals ==========

    def _dummy_logger(self):
        class _L:
            def debug(self, *a, **k): ...
            def info(self, *a, **k): ...
            def warning(self, *a, **k): ...
            def error(self, *a, **k): ...
            def exception(self, *a, **k): ...
        return _L()

    def _url(self, path: str) -> str:
        if not path.startswith("/"):
            path = "/" + path
        return self.base + path

    async def _get_json(self, path: str) -> Tuple[int, Any]:
        assert self._session, "Session is not started"
        url = self._url(path)
        self.log.debug("GET %s", url)
        async with self._session.get(url, timeout=self.timeout) as r:
            txt = await r.text()
            try:
                return r.status, json.loads(txt)
            except Exception:
                return r.status, {"raw": txt}

    async def _post_form(self, path: str, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[int, Any]:
        """
        3x-ui / 3X-UI ожидает x-www-form-urlencoded (или пустой POST для некоторых методов).
        """
        assert self._session, "Session is not started"
        url = self._url(path)
        self.log.debug("POST %s data=%r", url, data)
        async with self._session.post(url, data=data or None, timeout=self.timeout) as r:
            txt = await r.text()
            try:
                return r.status, json.loads(txt)
            except Exception:
                return r.status, {"raw": txt}

    @staticmethod
    def _safe_json_loads(val: Any) -> Any:
        if not isinstance(val, str):
            return val
        try:
            return json.loads(val)
        except Exception:
            return val

    # ========== API: inbounds ==========

    async def list_inbounds(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        GET /api/inbounds/list

        Поддержка разных форматов ответа:
          - [ ... ]
          - { obj: [ ... ] }
          - { data|rows|inbounds: [ ... ] }
          - вложенные obj/data.
        """
        st, body = await self._get_json("/api/inbounds/list")
        if st != 200:
            raise RuntimeError(f"inbounds/list HTTP {st}: {body}")

        # Уже список
        if isinstance(body, list):
            return body

        if isinstance(body, dict):
            # Официальный 3X-UI: { success, obj: [ ... ] }
            if isinstance(body.get("obj"), list):
                return body["obj"]

            # Другие ключи верхнего уровня
            for key in ("data", "rows", "inbounds"):
                if isinstance(body.get(key), list):
                    return body[key]

            # Вложенные варианты
            for outer in ("obj", "data"):
                inner = body.get(outer)
                if isinstance(inner, dict):
                    for key in ("obj", "data", "rows", "inbounds"):
                        if isinstance(inner.get(key), list):
                            return inner[key]

        raise RuntimeError(f"Unexpected inbounds/list payload: {body}")

    # ========== API: add client ==========

    async def add_client_append(
        self,
        inbound_id: Any,
        email: str,
        flow: str = "",
        sub_id: str = "",
        expiry_time: int = 0,
        *,
        client_id: Optional[str] = None,
        uuid: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        POST /api/inbounds/addClient

        Добавляет одного клиента в указанный инбаунд.

        expiry_time:
          - 0  => без ограничения
          - >0 => unix time в мс (как в панели 3x-ui)

        Параметры:
          - client_id: явный UUID клиента (строка). Если не передан — будет сгенерирован.
          - uuid: алиас для client_id (для совместимости со старым кодом).
        """
        # поддерживаем и client_id=, и uuid=, и автогенерацию
        cid = client_id or uuid or str(uuid_lib.uuid4())

        client_obj = {
            "id": cid,                       # фиксированный/общий UUID
            "email": email,
            "flow": flow or "",
            "limitIp": 0,
            "totalGB": 0,
            "expiryTime": int(expiry_time) if expiry_time else 0,
            "enable": True,
            "subId": sub_id,
            "tgId": 0,
            "reset": 0,
        }

        payload = {
            "id": str(inbound_id),
            "settings": json.dumps({"clients": [client_obj]}, ensure_ascii=False),
        }

        st, body = await self._post_form("/api/inbounds/addClient", payload)

        if st != 200:
            raise RuntimeError(f"addClient HTTP {st}: {body}")
        if not isinstance(body, dict) or not body.get("success"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"addClient failed: {body}")

        return body

    # ========== API: delete user clients ==========

    async def delete_user_clients(
        self,
        inbound: Dict[str, Any],
        username_clean: str,
        sub_id: str,
    ) -> int:
        """
        Удаляет всех клиентов пользователя из одного инбаунда.

        Совпадение по:
          - email (lowercase, без пробелов) начинается с username_clean.lower()
          - ИЛИ subId клиента == sub_id (в нижнем регистре)

        Использует:
          - POST /api/inbounds/{id}/delClient/{clientId}
          - fallback: POST /api/inbounds/delClient (id, clientId)
        """

        inbound_id = inbound.get("id")
        if inbound_id is None and isinstance(inbound.get("obj"), dict):
            inbound_id = inbound["obj"].get("id")
        if inbound_id is None:
            return 0

        settings = inbound.get("settings")
        obj = inbound.get("obj")
        if isinstance(obj, dict) and not settings:
            settings = obj.get("settings")

        settings = self._safe_json_loads(settings)
        settings = self._safe_json_loads(settings)  # на случай двойного JSON

        clients: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        if isinstance(settings, dict) and isinstance(settings.get("clients"), list):
            clients.extend(settings["clients"])

        if isinstance(inbound.get("clients"), list):
            clients.extend(inbound["clients"])

        if isinstance(obj, dict) and isinstance(obj.get("clients"), list):
            clients.extend(obj["clients"])

        if not clients:
            return 0

        # дедупликация
        uniq_clients: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        seen = set()
        for c in clients:
            if not isinstance(c, dict):
                continue
            cid = str(
                c.get("id") or c.get("uuid") or c.get("clientId") or ""
            ).strip()
            key = (cid, c.get("email"), c.get("subId"))
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            uniq_clients.append(c)
        clients = uniq_clients

        if not clients:
            return 0

        username_norm = (username_clean or "").lower().replace(" ", "")
        sub_id_norm = (sub_id or "").lower().strip()

        target_ids: List[str] = []

        for c in clients:
            cid = str(
                c.get("id") or c.get("uuid") or c.get("clientId") or ""
            ).strip()
            if not cid:
                continue

            email = str(c.get("email") or "")
            email_norm = email.lower().replace(" ", "")

            c_sub = str(
                c.get("subId") or c.get("subscription") or ""
            ).lower().strip()

            if (
                (username_norm and email_norm.startswith(username_norm))
                or (sub_id_norm and c_sub == sub_id_norm)
            ):
                target_ids.append(cid)

        if not target_ids:
            return 0

        deleted = 0
        for cid in target_ids:
            ok = False

            st, body = await self._post_form(
                f"/api/inbounds/{inbound_id}/delClient/{cid}",
                {},
            )
            if st == 200 and isinstance(body, dict) and body.get("success"):
                ok = True

            if not ok:
                st2, body2 = await self._post_form(
                    "/api/inbounds/delClient",
                    {"id": str(inbound_id), "clientId": cid},
                )
                if st2 == 200 and isinstance(body2, dict) and body2.get("success"):
                    ok = True
                else:
                    self.log.warning(
                        "delClient failed for inbound %s client %s: "
                        "HTTP %s resp=%r; fallback HTTP %s resp=%r",
                        inbound_id,
                        cid,
                        st,
                        body,
                        st2,
                        body2,
                    )

            if ok:
                deleted += 1

        return deleted

    # ========== API: restart Xray ==========

    async def restart_xray(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Перезапуск Xray через API 3X-UI.

        Официальный эндпоинт:
          POST /api/server/restartXrayService
        """
        st, body = await self._post_form("/api/server/restartXrayService", {})
        if st != 200:
            raise RuntimeError(f"restartXrayService HTTP {st}: {body}")
        if not isinstance(body, dict) or not body.get("success"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"restartXrayService failed: {body}")
        return body









































# handlers/location.py

from __future__ import annotations

from telegram import Update, InlineKeyboardMarkup, InlineKeyboardButton
from telegram.ext import ContextTypes

from config import db  # SubscriptionDB / обёртка над vpn_bot.db

# Коды локаций, которые храним в БД:
LOCATION_AUTO = "auto"
LOCATION_LT = "lt"
LOCATION_NL = "nl"


def _location_label(code: str) -> str:
    """Человеческая подпись для кода локации."""
    if code == LOCATION_LT:
        return "🇱🇹 Литва"
    if code == LOCATION_NL:
        return "🇳🇱 Нидерланды"
    return "🤖 Автовыбор"


def _build_location_text_and_keyboard(
    current: str,
) -> tuple[str, InlineKeyboardMarkup]:
    """
    Строим красивый текст + inline-клавиатуру с галочками возле выбранной локации.
    Автовыбор — по умолчанию и первой строкой.
    """
    if current not in (LOCATION_AUTO, LOCATION_LT, LOCATION_NL):
        current = LOCATION_AUTO

    current_label = _location_label(current)

    text = (
        f"🌍 <b>Текущая локация: {current_label}</b>\n\n"
        "После смены локации <b>переподключите Proxy</b>, "
        "чтобы обновить местоположение.\n\n"
        "Выберите желаемую локацию:\n\n"
        "<i>Изменения вступят в силу в течении минуты.</i>"
    )

    def mark(code: str) -> str:
        return "✅" if code == current else "▫️"

    # Автовыбор — САМАЯ ВЕРХНЯЯ строка
    keyboard = InlineKeyboardMarkup(
        inline_keyboard=[
            [
                InlineKeyboardButton(
                    text=f"{mark(LOCATION_AUTO)} 🤖 Автовыбор",
                    callback_data="loc_auto",
                )
            ],
            [
                InlineKeyboardButton(
                    text=f"{mark(LOCATION_LT)} 🇱🇹 Литва",
                    callback_data="loc_lt",
                )
            ],
            [
                InlineKeyboardButton(
                    text=f"{mark(LOCATION_NL)} 🇳🇱 Нидерланды",
                    callback_data="loc_nl",
                )
            ],
        ]
    )

    return text, keyboard


async def show_location_menu(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    """
    Показывает меню выбора локации.
    Вызывается из handlers/menu.py при нажатии на кнопку «Локации».
    ЕСЛИ тариф не premium — блокируем и предлагаем выбрать тариф.
    """
    user = update.effective_user
    if not user or not update.message:
        return

    tg_id = user.id

    # Проверяем тариф пользователя
    info = db.get_user(tg_id) or {}
    tariff = (info.get("tariff") or "").lower()

    if tariff != "premium":
        # Нет полного тарифа — показываем заглушку + кнопку "Выбрать тариф"
        text = (
            "🌍 Выбор конкретной локации доступен только для тарифа "
            "<b>💎 Premium</b>.\n\n"
            "С полным тарифом ты сможешь переключаться между серверами:\n"
            "• 🇱🇹 Литва\n"
            "• 🇳🇱 Нидерланды\n"
            "• 🤖 Автовыбор\n\n"
            "Подключи Premium тариф, чтобы разблокировать выбор локации."
        )

        keyboard = InlineKeyboardMarkup(
            [
                [
                    InlineKeyboardButton(
                        text="💎 Выбрать тариф",
                        callback_data="tariff_menu",  # обработает TariffService.handle_tariff_callback
                    )
                ]
            ]
        )

        await update.message.reply_text(
            text,
            parse_mode="HTML",
            reply_markup=keyboard,
        )
        return

    # Есть Premium тариф — показываем обычное меню локаций
    current = db.get_location_pref(tg_id)
    text, keyboard = _build_location_text_and_keyboard(current)

    await update.message.reply_text(
        text,
        parse_mode="HTML",
        reply_markup=keyboard,
    )


async def handle_location_callback(update: Update, context: ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    """
    Обработчик inline-кнопок локаций: loc_lt / loc_nl / loc_auto.
    Вызывается из _dispatch_callback в handlers/__init__.py.
    """
    query = update.callback_query
    if not query:
        return

    user = query.from_user
    if not user:
        await query.answer()
        return

    tg_id = user.id
    data = (query.data or "").strip()

    # На всякий случай: если вдруг тариф уже не premium, блокируем изменение
    info = db.get_user(tg_id) or {}
    tariff = (info.get("tariff") or "").lower()
    if tariff != "premium":
        await query.answer("Выбор локации доступен только для тарифа 💎 Premium", show_alert=True)
        return

    if data == "loc_lt":
        new_loc = LOCATION_LT
        toast = "Локация изменена: 🇱🇹 Литва"
    elif data == "loc_nl":
        new_loc = LOCATION_NL
        toast = "Локация изменена: 🇳🇱 Нидерланды"
    else:
        new_loc = LOCATION_AUTO
        toast = "Локация изменена: 🤖 Автовыбор"

    # Сохраняем в БД
    try:
        db.set_location_pref(tg_id, new_loc)
    except Exception:
        await query.answer("Ошибка при сохранении локации 😔", show_alert=True)
        return

    # Перестраиваем текст + клавиатуру с новой галочкой
    text, keyboard = _build_location_text_and_keyboard(new_loc)

    if query.message:
        await query.message.edit_text(
            text,
            parse_mode="HTML",
            reply_markup=keyboard,
        )

    await query.answer(toast)


































#!/usr/bin/env python3
# traffic_selector_dns_mapped.py

from __future__ import annotations
import os
import re
import sys
import asyncio
import time
import json
import logging
import signal
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple, Set
import statistics
import aiohttp
from datetime import datetime

# ---- Settings ----
SAMPLE_RATE = 5.0
WINDOW_SECS = 60.0
REPEAT_EVERY = 60.0
CONNECT_TIMEOUT = 1.5
HYSTERESIS_MIN_IMPROV = 0.08
HYSTERESIS_PERSIST = 2
SELECTED_OUT = "selected_location.txt"
ENV_FILENAME = ".env"

logging.basicConfig(level=os.environ.get("LOG_LEVEL", "INFO"), format="%(levelname)s:%(name)s:%(message)s")
LOG = logging.getLogger("selector_mapped")


# ---- Helpers ----
def load_env_file(fn: str) -> None:
    if not os.path.exists(fn):
        return
    try:
        with open(fn, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for raw in f:
                line = raw.strip()
                if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
                    continue
                k, v = line.split("=", 1)
                k = k.strip(); v = v.strip()
                if (v.startswith('"') and v.endswith('"')) or (v.startswith("'",) and v.endswith("'")):
                    v = v[1:-1]
                if k not in os.environ:
                    os.environ[k] = v
                    LOG.debug("Загружена %s из %s", k, fn)
    except Exception as e:
        LOG.warning("Ошибка загрузки %s: %s", fn, e)


def load_env_near_script():
    script_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    env1 = os.path.join(script_dir, ENV_FILENAME)
    load_env_file(env1)
    cwd = os.getcwd()
    if os.path.abspath(cwd) != os.path.abspath(script_dir):
        env2 = os.path.join(cwd, ENV_FILENAME)
        load_env_file(env2)


def parse_csv(s: Optional[str]) -> List[str]:
    if not s:
        return []
    return [p.strip() for p in s.split(",") if p.strip()]


def _choose_status_url(base: str) -> str:
    b = base.rstrip("/")
    if b.endswith("/panel"):
        return f"{b}/api/server/status"
    return f"{b}/panel/api/server/status"


def bytes_to_human(bps: float) -> str:
    units = ["B/s", "KB/s", "MB/s", "GB/s"]
    val = float(bps)
    i = 0
    while val >= 1024 and i < len(units)-1:
        val /= 1024.0
        i += 1
    return f"{val:.2f} {units[i]}"


def format_timestamp(ts: Any) -> str:
    """Convert timestamp to readable format"""
    if not ts:
        return "N/A"
    try:
        if isinstance(ts, str):
            ts = int(ts)
        # Handle milliseconds timestamp
        if ts > 1000000000000:  # Likely milliseconds
            ts = ts / 1000
        dt = datetime.fromtimestamp(ts)
        return dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OSError) as e:
        return f"Invalid timestamp: {ts}"


def bytes_to_gb(bytes_val: int) -> float:
    """Convert bytes to GB"""
    return bytes_val / (1024 ** 3)


def bytes_to_mb(bytes_val: int) -> float:
    """Convert bytes to MB"""
    return bytes_val / (1024 ** 2)


# ---- Main selector ----
class TrafficSelectorMapped:
    def __init__(self):
        load_env_near_script()
        self.servers = self._discover_panels()
        self.panel_names_sorted = sorted(self.servers.keys(), key=self._panel_index)
        self.mapping = self._build_panel_to_ip_mapping()
        self._session: Optional[aiohttp.ClientSession] = None
        self._stop = False
        self.current_choice: Optional[str] = None
        self._candidate_count: Dict[str, int] = {}
        self._prev_scores: Dict[str, float] = {}

    def _panel_index(self, name: str) -> int:
        m = re.search(r"(\d+)$", name)
        return int(m.group(1)) if m else 0

    def _discover_panels(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, str]]:
        pattern = re.compile(r"^XUI_BASE_(\d+)$")
        servers = {}
        for k, v in list(os.environ.items()):
            m = pattern.match(k)
            if not m:
                continue
            idx = m.group(1)
            base = v.strip()
            cookie = os.environ.get(f"XUI_COOKIE_{idx}", "").strip()
            name = f"panel_{idx}"
            if not cookie:
                LOG.warning("Найден %s, но XUI_COOKIE_%s не задан — пропускаем", name, idx)
                continue
            servers[name] = {"base": base, "cookie": cookie}
        return servers

    def _build_panel_to_ip_mapping(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        p2ip_raw = os.environ.get("PANEL_TO_IP")
        if p2ip_raw:
            try:
                parts = [p.strip() for p in p2ip_raw.split(",") if p.strip()]
                mapping = {}
                for part in parts:
                    if "=" in part:
                        pn, ip = part.split("=", 1)
                        mapping[pn.strip()] = ip.strip()
                mapping = {pn: ip for pn, ip in mapping.items() if pn in self.servers}
                if mapping:
                    LOG.info("Используется PANEL_TO_IP из окружения: %s", mapping)
                    return mapping
            except Exception as e:
                LOG.warning("Не удалось распарсить PANEL_TO_IP: %s", e)

        lt = os.environ.get("KINGLINE_LT_IP") or os.environ.get("KINGLINEKINGLINE_LT_IP")
        nl = os.environ.get("KINGLINE_NL_IP")
        ordered_panels = sorted(self.servers.keys(), key=self._panel_index)
        mapping = {}
        if lt and nl and len(ordered_panels) >= 2:
            mapping[ordered_panels[0]] = lt
            mapping[ordered_panels[1]] = nl
            for i, pn in enumerate(ordered_panels[2:], start=2):
                mapping[pn] = lt if (i % 2 == 0) else nl
            LOG.info("Используется KINGLINE_LT_IP/KINGLINE_NL_IP сопоставление: %s", mapping)
            return mapping

        dns_ips = parse_csv(os.environ.get("KINGLINE_DNS_IPS") or "")
        if dns_ips and ordered_panels:
            for pn, ip in zip(ordered_panels, dns_ips):
                mapping[pn] = ip
            if len(ordered_panels) > len(dns_ips):
                last_ip = dns_ips[-1]
                for pn in ordered_panels[len(dns_ips):]:
                    mapping[pn] = last_ip
            LOG.info("Используется KINGLINE_DNS_IPS сопоставление: %s", mapping)
            return mapping

        for pn in ordered_panels:
            base = self.servers[pn]["base"]
            m = re.match(r"https?://([^:/]+)", base)
            if m:
                mapping[pn] = m.group(1)
        if mapping:
            LOG.info("Используется извлечение хостов из XUI_BASE_*: %s", mapping)
            return mapping

        LOG.warning("Не удалось автоматически сопоставить панели и IP. Задайте PANEL_TO_IP в .env")
        return mapping

    async def _ensure_session(self):
        if not self._session:
            self._session = aiohttp.ClientSession(headers={
                "Accept": "application/json, text/plain, */*",
                "X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest",
                "User-Agent": "selector-mapped/1.0",
            })

    async def _fetch_status(self, base: str, cookie: str, timeout: int = 10) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        await self._ensure_session()
        url = _choose_status_url(base)
        try:
            async with self._session.get(url, headers={"Cookie": cookie}, timeout=timeout) as r:
                txt = await r.text()
                try:
                    data = json.loads(txt)
                except Exception:
                    LOG.debug("Не JSON от %s: %s", url, txt[:200])
                    return {}
                if isinstance(data, dict) and "obj" in data and isinstance(data["obj"], dict):
                    return data["obj"]
                if isinstance(data, dict):
                    return data
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.warning("Ошибка запроса статуса %s: %s", base, e)
        return {}

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_bytes(obj: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[int, int]:
        up = down = 0
        try:
            nio = obj.get("netIO", {})
            if isinstance(nio, dict):
                up = int(nio.get("up", up) or up)
                down = int(nio.get("down", down) or down)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if up == 0 and down == 0:
            try:
                nt = obj.get("netTraffic", {})
                if isinstance(nt, dict):
                    up = int(nt.get("sent", up) or up)
                    down = int(nt.get("recv", down) or down)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return int(up or 0), int(down or 0)

    async def _measure_latency(self, host: str, port: int = 443, timeout: float = CONNECT_TIMEOUT) -> float:
        start = time.monotonic()
        try:
            fut = asyncio.open_connection(host, port)
            coro = asyncio.wait_for(fut, timeout=timeout)
            reader, writer = await coro
            try:
                writer.close()
                await writer.wait_closed()
            except Exception:
                pass
            return max(0.0, time.monotonic() - start)
        except Exception:
            return -1.0  # недоступен

    async def _sample_panel_stats(self, pn: str, cfg: Dict[str, str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        samples = []
        obj = await self._fetch_status(cfg["base"], cfg["cookie"])
        up0, down0 = self._extract_bytes(obj)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        last_up, last_down = up0, down0
        last_t = t0
        end = t0 + WINDOW_SECS
        while time.monotonic() < end and not self._stop:
            await asyncio.sleep(SAMPLE_RATE)
            o = await self._fetch_status(cfg["base"], cfg["cookie"])
            upn, downn = self._extract_bytes(o)
            tn = time.monotonic()
            delta = max(0, (upn - last_up) + (downn - last_down))
            dt = max(1e-6, tn - last_t)
            inst = delta / dt
            samples.append(inst)
            last_up, last_down = upn, downn
            last_t = tn
        if not samples:
            return {"avg": 0.0, "peak": 0.0, "stddev": 0.0, "count": 0}
        return {"avg": statistics.mean(samples), "peak": max(samples), "stddev": statistics.pstdev(samples), "count": len(samples)}

    async def _fetch_with_cookie(self, url: str, cookie: str, timeout: int = 10) -> Any:
        await self._ensure_session()
        try:
            async with self._session.get(url, headers={"Cookie": cookie}, timeout=timeout) as r:
                txt = await r.text()
                try:
                    return json.loads(txt)
                except Exception:
                    LOG.debug("Не JSON от %s: %s", url, txt[:200])
                    return {}
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.warning("Ошибка запроса %s: %s", url, e)
            return {}

    async def _post_with_cookie(self, url: str, cookie: str, data: Optional[Dict] = None, timeout: int = 10) -> Any:
        await self._ensure_session()
        try:
            async with self._session.post(url, headers={"Cookie": cookie}, json=data, timeout=timeout) as r:
                txt = await r.text()
                try:
                    return json.loads(txt)
                except Exception:
                    LOG.debug("Не JSON от %s: %s", url, txt[:200])
                    return {}
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.warning("Ошибка POST запроса %s: %s", url, e)
            return {}

    # === COMPREHENSIVE CLIENT DATA COLLECTION ===

    async def _get_all_inbounds(self, base: str, cookie: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get all inbounds with full configuration"""
        url = f"{base.rstrip('/')}/api/inbounds/list"
        data = await self._fetch_with_cookie(url, cookie)
        if isinstance(data, dict) and "obj" in data and isinstance(data["obj"], list):
            return data["obj"]
        return []

    async def _get_inbound_by_id(self, base: str, cookie: str, inbound_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get detailed inbound information by ID"""
        url = f"{base.rstrip('/')}/api/inbounds/get/{inbound_id}"
        data = await self._fetch_with_cookie(url, cookie)
        if isinstance(data, dict) and "obj" in data:
            return data["obj"]
        return {}

    async def _get_client_traffics_by_email(self, base: str, cookie: str, email: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get client traffic statistics by email"""
        url = f"{base.rstrip('/')}/api/inbounds/getClientTraffics/{email}"
        data = await self._fetch_with_cookie(url, cookie)
        LOG.debug(f"Traffic data for {email}: {data}")
        if isinstance(data, dict) and "obj" in data:
            return data["obj"]
        return {}

    async def _get_client_traffics_by_inbound_id(self, base: str, cookie: str, inbound_id: int) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get traffic statistics for all clients in an inbound"""
        url = f"{base.rstrip('/')}/api/inbounds/getClientTrafficsById/{inbound_id}"
        data = await self._fetch_with_cookie(url, cookie)
        LOG.debug(f"Traffic data for inbound {inbound_id}: {data}")
        if isinstance(data, dict) and "obj" in data and isinstance(data["obj"], list):
            return data["obj"]
        return []

    async def _get_client_ips(self, base: str, cookie: str, email: str) -> List[str]:
        """Get client IP addresses"""
        url = f"{base.rstrip('/')}/api/inbounds/clientIps/{email}"
        data = await self._post_with_cookie(url, cookie)
        if isinstance(data, dict) and "obj" in data and isinstance(data["obj"], list):
            return data["obj"]
        return []

    async def _get_online_clients(self, base: str, cookie: str) -> List[str]:
        """Get currently online clients (email list)"""
        url = f"{base.rstrip('/')}/api/inbounds/onlines"
        data = await self._post_with_cookie(url, cookie)
        if isinstance(data, dict) and "obj" in data and isinstance(data["obj"], list):
            return data["obj"]
        if isinstance(data, list):
            return data
        return []

    async def _get_last_online(self, base: str, cookie: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get last online status of all clients"""
        url = f"{base.rstrip('/')}/api/inbounds/lastOnline"
        data = await self._post_with_cookie(url, cookie)
        if isinstance(data, dict) and "obj" in data:
            return data["obj"]
        return {}

    async def _get_inbound_client_stats(self, base: str, cookie: str, inbound_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get detailed client statistics for a specific inbound"""
        try:
            # Get the full inbound data
            inbound_data = await self._get_inbound_by_id(base, cookie, inbound_id)
            if not inbound_data:
                return {}
            
            # Extract client settings
            clients_from_settings = await self._extract_clients_from_inbound(inbound_data)
            
            # Get traffic data for this inbound
            client_traffics = await self._get_client_traffics_by_inbound_id(base, cookie, inbound_id)
            
            # Create a mapping of email to traffic data
            traffic_map = {}
            for client_traffic in client_traffics:
                if isinstance(client_traffic, dict):
                    email = client_traffic.get('email')
                    if email:
                        traffic_map[email] = {
                            'up': client_traffic.get('up', 0),
                            'down': client_traffic.get('down', 0),
                            'total': client_traffic.get('up', 0) + client_traffic.get('down', 0),
                            'enable': client_traffic.get('enable', True)
                        }
            
            # Combine client settings with traffic data
            detailed_clients = []
            for client_settings in clients_from_settings:
                client_email = client_settings.get('email') or client_settings.get('id') or client_settings.get('password')
                if not client_email:
                    continue
                    
                # Get individual traffic data as fallback
                individual_traffic = await self._get_client_traffics_by_email(base, cookie, client_email)
                
                # Use traffic from inbound if available, otherwise use individual data
                traffic_data = traffic_map.get(client_email, {})
                if not traffic_data and individual_traffic:
                    traffic_data = {
                        'up': individual_traffic.get('up', 0),
                        'down': individual_traffic.get('down', 0),
                        'total': individual_traffic.get('up', 0) + individual_traffic.get('down', 0),
                        'enable': individual_traffic.get('enable', True)
                    }
                
                # Get online status
                online_clients = await self._get_online_clients(base, cookie)
                last_online_data = await self._get_last_online(base, cookie)
                
                detailed_client = {
                    'email': client_email,
                    'settings': client_settings,
                    'traffic': traffic_data,
                    'is_online': client_email in online_clients,
                    'last_online': last_online_data.get(client_email) if last_online_data else None,
                    'ips': await self._get_client_ips(base, cookie, client_email)
                }
                detailed_clients.append(detailed_client)
            
            return {
                'inbound_id': inbound_id,
                'protocol': inbound_data.get('protocol', 'N/A'),
                'port': inbound_data.get('port', 'N/A'),
                'tag': inbound_data.get('tag', 'N/A'),
                'total_up': inbound_data.get('up', 0),
                'total_down': inbound_data.get('down', 0),
                'total_traffic': inbound_data.get('up', 0) + inbound_data.get('down', 0),
                'clients': detailed_clients
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.error(f"Error getting inbound {inbound_id} client stats: {e}")
            return {}

    async def _extract_clients_from_inbound(self, inbound_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Extract client information from inbound settings"""
        clients = []
        try:
            settings = inbound_data.get('settings', '')
            if isinstance(settings, str):
                try:
                    settings_obj = json.loads(settings)
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    settings_obj = {}
            else:
                settings_obj = settings

            # Extract clients based on protocol
            protocol = inbound_data.get('protocol', '').lower()
            
            if protocol in ['vmess', 'vless']:
                clients_data = settings_obj.get('clients', [])
                for client in clients_data:
                    if isinstance(client, dict):
                        clients.append({
                            "id": client.get('id'),
                            "email": client.get('email'),
                            "flow": client.get('flow'),
                            "enable": client.get('enable', True),
                            "totalGB": client.get('totalGB', 0),
                            "expiryTime": client.get('expiryTime', 0),
                            "protocol": protocol
                        })
            
            elif protocol == 'trojan':
                clients_data = settings_obj.get('clients', [])
                for client in clients_data:
                    if isinstance(client, dict):
                        clients.append({
                            "password": client.get('password'),
                            "email": client.get('email'),
                            "flow": client.get('flow'),
                            "enable": client.get('enable', True),
                            "totalGB": client.get('totalGB', 0),
                            "expiryTime": client.get('expiryTime', 0),
                            "protocol": protocol
                        })
            
            elif protocol == 'shadowsocks':
                clients_data = settings_obj.get('clients', [])
                for client in clients_data:
                    if isinstance(client, dict):
                        clients.append({
                            "email": client.get('email'),
                            "password": client.get('password'),
                            "enable": client.get('enable', True),
                            "totalGB": client.get('totalGB', 0),
                            "expiryTime": client.get('expiryTime', 0),
                            "protocol": protocol
                        })
            
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.debug("Ошибка извлечения клиентов из inbound: %s", e)
        
        return clients

    async def _get_all_clients_data(self, base: str, cookie: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get comprehensive data for all clients across all inbounds"""
        all_data = {
            "panel_base": base,
            "total_inbounds": 0,
            "total_clients": 0,
            "online_clients": 0,
            "inbounds": [],
            "traffic_summary": {
                "total_up": 0,
                "total_down": 0,
                "total_traffic": 0
            }
        }

        try:
            # Get all inbounds
            inbounds = await self._get_all_inbounds(base, cookie)
            all_data["total_inbounds"] = len(inbounds)
            
            # Get online clients
            online_clients = await self._get_online_clients(base, cookie)
            
            # Process each inbound
            inbound_tasks = []
            for inbound in inbounds:
                if not isinstance(inbound, dict):
                    continue
                    
                inbound_id = inbound.get('id')
                if inbound_id:
                    inbound_tasks.append(self._get_inbound_client_stats(base, cookie, inbound_id))
            
            # Wait for all inbound data
            inbound_results = await asyncio.gather(*inbound_tasks, return_exceptions=True)
            
            all_clients_set = set()
            
            for inbound_result in inbound_results:
                if isinstance(inbound_result, Exception):
                    LOG.error(f"Error processing inbound: {inbound_result}")
                    continue
                    
                if not inbound_result:
                    continue
                
                # Add to traffic summary
                all_data["traffic_summary"]["total_up"] += inbound_result.get('total_up', 0)
                all_data["traffic_summary"]["total_down"] += inbound_result.get('total_down', 0)
                all_data["traffic_summary"]["total_traffic"] += inbound_result.get('total_traffic', 0)
                
                # Count online clients
                for client in inbound_result.get('clients', []):
                    client_email = client.get('email')
                    if client_email:
                        all_clients_set.add(client_email)
                        if client.get('is_online'):
                            all_data["online_clients"] += 1
                
                all_data["inbounds"].append(inbound_result)
            
            all_data["total_clients"] = len(all_clients_set)
            
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.error("Ошибка получения всех данных клиентов: %s", e)
        
        return all_data

    async def evaluate_clients(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Comprehensive client evaluation for all panels"""
        await self._ensure_session()
        results = {}
        
        tasks = {
            pn: asyncio.create_task(self._get_all_clients_data(cfg["base"], cfg["cookie"]))
            for pn, cfg in self.servers.items()
        }
        
        for panel_name, task in tasks.items():
            try:
                results[panel_name] = await task
                LOG.info(f"Панель {panel_name}: собраны данные по {results[panel_name].get('total_clients', 0)} клиентам в {results[panel_name].get('total_inbounds', 0)} inbound'ах")
            except Exception as e:
                LOG.warning(f"Ошибка сбора клиентов для {panel_name}: {e}")
                results[panel_name] = {
                    "panel_base": self.servers[panel_name]["base"],
                    "total_inbounds": 0,
                    "total_clients": 0,
                    "online_clients": 0,
                    "inbounds": [],
                    "traffic_summary": {"total_up": 0, "total_down": 0, "total_traffic": 0}
                }
        
        return results

    async def evaluate(self) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]], Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]]:
        await self._ensure_session()
        tasks = {pn: asyncio.create_task(self._sample_panel_stats(pn, cfg)) for pn, cfg in self.servers.items()}
        panel_stats = {}
        for pn, task in tasks.items():
            try:
                panel_stats[pn] = await task
            except Exception as e:
                LOG.warning("Ошибка сбора для %s: %s", pn, e)
                panel_stats[pn] = {"avg": 0.0, "peak": 0.0, "stddev": 0.0, "count": 0}

        per_ip = {}
        for pn, stats in panel_stats.items():
            ip = self.mapping.get(pn)
            if not ip:
                continue
            rec = per_ip.get(ip, {"avg": 0.0, "peak": 0.0, "stddev_vals": [], "count": 0})
            rec["avg"] += stats["avg"]
            rec["peak"] += stats["peak"]
            rec["stddev_vals"].append(stats["stddev"])
            rec["count"] += stats["count"]
            per_ip[ip] = rec

        lat_tasks = {ip: asyncio.create_task(self._measure_latency(ip)) for ip in per_ip.keys()}
        lat_results = {}
        for ip, t in lat_tasks.items():
            lat = await t
            if lat < 0:
                LOG.warning("IP %s недоступен — исключаем из кандидатов", ip)
            lat_results[ip] = lat

        max_avg = max((rec["avg"] for rec in per_ip.values()), default=1.0)
        max_peak = max((rec["peak"] for rec in per_ip.values()), default=1.0)
        max_lat = max((lat_results.get(ip, CONNECT_TIMEOUT+2.0) for ip in per_ip.keys()), default=(CONNECT_TIMEOUT+2.0))
        w_avg = 0.6; w_peak = 0.25; w_lat = 0.15

        per_ip_stats = {}
        for ip, rec in per_ip.items():
            if lat_results.get(ip, -1) < 0:
                continue  # исключаем недоступные
            avg = rec["avg"]
            peak = rec["peak"]
            stddev = statistics.mean(rec["stddev_vals"]) if rec["stddev_vals"] else 0.0
            lat = lat_results.get(ip, CONNECT_TIMEOUT+2.0)
            avg_n = avg / (max_avg or 1)
            peak_n = peak / (max_peak or 1)
            lat_n = lat / (max_lat or 1)
            score = w_avg * avg_n + w_peak * peak_n + w_lat * lat_n
            per_ip_stats[ip] = {"avg": avg, "peak": peak, "stddev": stddev, "latency": lat, "score": score}

        return per_ip_stats, panel_stats

    def _choose_best_with_hysteresis(self, per_ip_stats: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]) -> Optional[str]:
        if not per_ip_stats:
            return None
        sorted_ips = sorted(per_ip_stats.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]["score"])
        best_ip, best_data = sorted_ips[0]
        best_score = best_data["score"]
        prev = self.current_choice
        if prev is None:
            self.current_choice = best_ip
            self._candidate_count = {best_ip: 1}
            self._prev_scores[best_ip] = best_score
            LOG.info("Первичный выбор: %s (score=%.4f)", best_ip, best_score)
            return best_ip
        if best_ip == prev:
            self._prev_scores[best_ip] = best_score
            self._candidate_count[best_ip] = 0
            return best_ip
        prev_score = self._prev_scores.get(prev, 1.0)
        improvement = (prev_score - best_score) / max(prev_score, 1e-6)
        if improvement >= HYSTERESIS_MIN_IMPROV:
            LOG.info("Новый %s лучше текущего %s (улучшение %.2f%%) — переключаемся", best_ip, prev, improvement*100)
            self.current_choice = best_ip
            self._prev_scores[best_ip] = best_score
            self._candidate_count = {best_ip: 0}
            return best_ip
        cnt = self._candidate_count.get(best_ip, 0) + 1
        self._candidate_count[best_ip] = cnt
        if cnt >= HYSTERESIS_PERSIST:
            LOG.info("Кандидат %s держится %d раз(а) подряд -> переключаемся с %s", best_ip, cnt, prev)
            self.current_choice = best_ip
            self._prev_scores[best_ip] = best_score
            self._candidate_count = {best_ip: 0}
            return best_ip
        LOG.info("Кандидат %s пока не стабилен (cnt=%d/%d) — остаёмся на %s", best_ip, cnt, HYSTERESIS_PERSIST, prev)
        return prev

    def _persist_choice(self, ip: str):
        try:
            script_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
            fn = os.path.join(script_dir, SELECTED_OUT)
            with open(fn, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(ip + "\n")
            LOG.info("Записали выбранный IP в %s: %s", fn, ip)
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.warning("Не удалось записать выбор: %s", e)

    def _format_comprehensive_client_report(self, client_stats: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
        """Format comprehensive client report"""
        report = []
        report.append("=== ПОЛНАЯ СТАТИСТИКА ПО КЛИЕНТАМ И INBOUND'АМ ===\n")
        
        for panel_name, data in client_stats.items():
            report.append(f"┌─────────────────────────────────────────────")
            report.append(f"│ Панель: {panel_name}")
            report.append(f"│ URL: {data.get('panel_base', 'N/A')}")
            report.append(f"│ Всего inbound'ов: {data.get('total_inbounds', 0)}")
            report.append(f"│ Всего клиентов: {data.get('total_clients', 0)}")
            report.append(f"│ Онлайн клиентов: {data.get('online_clients', 0)}")
            
            traffic = data.get('traffic_summary', {})
            report.append(f"│ Общий трафик: {bytes_to_gb(traffic.get('total_traffic', 0)):.2f} GB")
            report.append(f"│   ↑ Отправлено: {bytes_to_gb(traffic.get('total_up', 0)):.2f} GB")
            report.append(f"│   ↓ Получено: {bytes_to_gb(traffic.get('total_down', 0)):.2f} GB")
            report.append(f"└─────────────────────────────────────────────\n")
            
            # Inbounds details
            inbounds = data.get('inbounds', [])
            for inbound in inbounds:
                report.append(f"  ┌─ Inbound #{inbound.get('inbound_id', 'N/A')}")
                report.append(f"  │ Протокол: {inbound.get('protocol', 'N/A')}")
                report.append(f"  │ Порт: {inbound.get('port', 'N/A')}")
                report.append(f"  │ Тег: {inbound.get('tag', 'N/A')}")
                report.append(f"  │ Клиентов: {len(inbound.get('clients', []))}")
                
                inbound_traffic = inbound.get('total_traffic', 0)
                report.append(f"  │ Трафик инбаунда: {bytes_to_gb(inbound_traffic):.2f} GB")
                report.append(f"  └─────────────────────────────────────────────")
                
                # Clients in this inbound
                clients = inbound.get('clients', [])
                for i, client in enumerate(clients, 1):
                    report.append(f"     {i}. {client.get('email', 'N/A')}")
                    
                    # Traffic data - ОСНОВНОЕ ИЗМЕНЕНИЕ
                    traffic_data = client.get('traffic', {})
                    if traffic_data:
                        up = traffic_data.get('up', 0)
                        down = traffic_data.get('down', 0)
                        total = traffic_data.get('total', 0)
                        
                        # Always show traffic, even if zero
                        if total == 0:
                            report.append(f"        📊 Трафик: ↑0.00MB ↓0.00MB Σ0.00MB")
                        elif total < 1024**3:  # Less than 1GB
                            report.append(f"        📊 Трафик: ↑{bytes_to_mb(up):.2f}MB ↓{bytes_to_mb(down):.2f}MB Σ{bytes_to_mb(total):.2f}MB")
                        else:
                            report.append(f"        📊 Трафик: ↑{bytes_to_gb(up):.2f}GB ↓{bytes_to_gb(down):.2f}GB Σ{bytes_to_gb(total):.2f}GB")
                    else:
                        report.append(f"        📊 Трафик: данные недоступны")
                    
                    # Online status
                    status_icon = "🟢" if client.get('is_online') else "⚫"
                    last_online = client.get('last_online')
                    last_online_str = format_timestamp(last_online) if last_online else "N/A"
                    report.append(f"        📱 Статус: {status_icon} {last_online_str}")
                    
                    # IP addresses
                    ips = client.get('ips', [])
                    if ips:
                        report.append(f"        🌐 IP: {', '.join(ips[:3])}" + 
                                    ("..." if len(ips) > 3 else ""))
                    
                    # Client configuration from settings
                    settings = client.get('settings', {})
                    if settings.get('totalGB', 0) > 0:
                        report.append(f"        ⚡ Лимит: {settings.get('totalGB', 0)} GB")
                    if settings.get('expiryTime', 0) > 0:
                        report.append(f"        📅 Истекает: {format_timestamp(settings.get('expiryTime', 0))}")
                    
                    report.append(f"        🔗 Протокол: {settings.get('protocol', 'N/A')}")
                    report.append("")
            
            report.append("")  # Empty line between panels
        
        return "\n".join(report)

    async def run_once(self):
        per_ip_stats, panel_stats = await self.evaluate()
        ts = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", time.localtime())
        print(f"=== Отчёт выбора @ {ts} ===")
        print("Статистика по панелям:")
        for pn, st in panel_stats.items():
            print(f"  {pn}: avg={bytes_to_human(st['avg'])}  peak={bytes_to_human(st['peak'])}  samples={st['count']}")
        print("Статистика по локациям (IP):")
        for ip, st in per_ip_stats.items():
            print(f"  {ip}: latency={st['latency']:.3f}s  avg={bytes_to_human(st['avg'])}  peak={bytes_to_human(st['peak'])}  score={st['score']:.4f}")
        if not per_ip_stats:
            LOG.warning("Нет доступных локаций для выбора.")
            return
        chosen = self._choose_best_with_hysteresis(per_ip_stats)
        if chosen:
            print(f"-> Рекомендуем подключаться к: {chosen}")
            self._persist_choice(chosen)
        else:
            LOG.warning("Нет доступного сервера для рекомендации.")
        
        # Comprehensive client data collection
        print("\nСбор полной статистики по клиентам и inbound'ам...")
        client_stats = await self.evaluate_clients()
        
        # Format and display comprehensive report
        comprehensive_report = self._format_comprehensive_client_report(client_stats)
        print(comprehensive_report)
        
        # Save detailed report to file
        try:
            script_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
            report_file = os.path.join(script_dir, "detailed_client_report.txt")
            with open(report_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(f"Отчёт сгенерирован: {ts}\n\n")
                f.write(comprehensive_report)
            print(f"\nПолный детальный отчет сохранен в: {report_file}")
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.warning("Не удалось сохранить детальный отчет: %s", e)

    async def start_loop(self):
        try:
            while not self._stop:
                await self.run_once()
                waited = 0.0
                while waited < REPEAT_EVERY and not self._stop:
                    await asyncio.sleep(1.0)
                    waited += 1.0
        finally:
            if self._session:
                await self._session.close()

    async def stop(self):
        self._stop = True
        if self._session:
            await self._session.close()


def main():
    sel = TrafficSelectorMapped()
    loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
    asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)

    def _sig(signum, frame):
        LOG.info("Получен сигнал %s — останавливаемся...", signum)
        loop.create_task(sel.stop())

    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, _sig)
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, _sig)

    try:
        loop.run_until_complete(sel.start_loop())
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        LOG.info("Остановка по Ctrl-C")
    finally:
        loop.run_until_complete(sel.stop())
        loop.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()























#!/usr/bin/env python3
# traffic_monitor.py

from __future__ import annotations
import os
import re
import asyncio
import time
import json
import logging
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple
import statistics
import aiohttp
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

# ---- Settings ----
SAMPLE_RATE = 5.0
WINDOW_SECS = 60.0
CONNECT_TIMEOUT = 1.5
DATA_FILE = "traffic_data.json"
LOG_LEVEL = "INFO"

logging.basicConfig(level=LOG_LEVEL, format="%(levelname)s:%(name)s:%(message)s")
LOG = logging.getLogger("traffic_monitor")


class TrafficMonitor:
    def __init__(self):
        self.servers = self._discover_panels()
        self._session: Optional[aiohttp.ClientSession] = None
        self._stop = False
        self._last_cleanup = time.time()

    def _discover_panels(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, str]]:
        pattern = re.compile(r"^XUI_BASE_(\d+)$")
        servers = {}
        for k, v in list(os.environ.items()):
            m = pattern.match(k)
            if not m:
                continue
            idx = m.group(1)
            base = v.strip()
            cookie = os.environ.get(f"XUI_COOKIE_{idx}", "").strip()
            name = f"panel_{idx}"
            if not cookie:
                LOG.warning("Найден %s, но XUI_COOKIE_%s не задан — пропускаем", name, idx)
                continue
            servers[name] = {"base": base, "cookie": cookie}
        return servers

    async def _ensure_session(self):
        if not self._session:
            self._session = aiohttp.ClientSession(headers={
                "Accept": "application/json, text/plain, */*",
                "X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest",
                "User-Agent": "traffic-monitor/1.0",
            })

    async def _fetch_status(self, base: str, cookie: str, timeout: int = 10) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        await self._ensure_session()
        url = self._choose_status_url(base)
        try:
            async with self._session.get(url, headers={"Cookie": cookie}, timeout=timeout) as r:
                txt = await r.text()
                try:
                    data = json.loads(txt)
                except Exception:
                    LOG.debug("Не JSON от %s: %s", url, txt[:200])
                    return {}
                if isinstance(data, dict) and "obj" in data and isinstance(data["obj"], dict):
                    return data["obj"]
                if isinstance(data, dict):
                    return data
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.warning("Ошибка запроса статуса %s: %s", base, e)
        return {}

    def _choose_status_url(self, base: str) -> str:
        b = base.rstrip("/")
        if b.endswith("/panel"):
            return f"{b}/api/server/status"
        return f"{b}/panel/api/server/status"

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_bytes(obj: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[int, int]:
        up = down = 0
        try:
            nio = obj.get("netIO", {})
            if isinstance(nio, dict):
                up = int(nio.get("up", up) or up)
                down = int(nio.get("down", down) or down)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if up == 0 and down == 0:
            try:
                nt = obj.get("netTraffic", {})
                if isinstance(nt, dict):
                    up = int(nt.get("sent", up) or up)
                    down = int(nt.get("recv", down) or down)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return int(up or 0), int(down or 0)

    async def _get_online_clients(self, base: str, cookie: str) -> int:
        """Get number of online clients"""
        url = f"{base.rstrip('/')}/api/inbounds/onlines"
        try:
            await self._ensure_session()
            async with self._session.post(url, headers={"Cookie": cookie}, timeout=10) as r:
                txt = await r.text()
                try:
                    data = json.loads(txt)
                    if isinstance(data, dict) and "obj" in data and isinstance(data["obj"], list):
                        return len(data["obj"])
                    if isinstance(data, list):
                        return len(data)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        except Exception as e:
            LOG.debug("Ошибка получения онлайн клиентов: %s", e)
        return 0

    async def _measure_latency(self, host: str) -> float:
        """Measure connection latency to server"""
        try:
            # Extract hostname from URL
            if "://" in host:
                host = host.split("://")[1].split("/")[0]
            # Remove port if present
            host = host.split(":")[0]
            
            start = time.monotonic()
            fut = asyncio.open_connection(host, 443)
            coro = asyncio.wait_for(fut, timeout=CONNECT_TIMEOUT)
            reader, writer = await coro
            latency = time.monotonic() - start
            writer.close()
            await writer.wait_closed()
            return max(0.0, latency)
        except Exception:
            return -1.0

    async def _measure_speed(self, cfg: Dict[str, str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Measure speed statistics over a short period"""
        samples = []
        obj = await self._fetch_status(cfg["base"], cfg["cookie"])
        up0, down0 = self._extract_bytes(obj)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        last_up, last_down = up0, down0
        last_t = t0
        end = t0 + 10  # Measure for 10 seconds for quicker results
        
        while time.monotonic() < end and not self._stop:
            await asyncio.sleep(2)  # Sample every 2 seconds
            o = await self._fetch_status(cfg["base"], cfg["cookie"])
            upn, downn = self._extract_bytes(o)
            tn = time.monotonic()
            delta = max(0, (upn - last_up) + (downn - last_down))
            dt = max(1e-6, tn - last_t)
            inst = delta / dt
            samples.append(inst)
            last_up, last_down = upn, downn
            last_t = tn
        
        if not samples:
